24/02/2026, 12:11 - Hansika Didi: SBI minor account, Pehla Kadam and Pehli Udaan, came into action on November 7, 2017 ¹.

Pehla Kadam is for kids below 18 years, and Pehli Udaan is for kids above 10 years. These accounts help inculcate savings habits in kids and teach them about modern banking ¹ ².

Do you want to know more about the features or eligibility criteria for these accounts?
24/02/2026, 12:12 - samarth: @⁨Meta AI⁩ like could they open bank account then too?
24/02/2026, 12:12 - Hansika Didi: You're asking if kids can open a bank account under Pehla Kadam or Pehli Udaan schemes even if they're below 10 years? 😊

Yes, they can! Pehla Kadam is for kids below 18 years, so even infants can have an account opened by their parents or guardians. For Pehli Udaan, it's for kids above 10 years, and they can operate it independently with parental consent.

Want to know more about the documents required or how to open one?

*ऑनलाइन आवेदन करने के लिए आवश्यक दस्तावेज:*
